def adjoint_auto(a,p):
  n=len(a); pmax=p.max()
  if pmax > 0.95:
     # get top 16
     k=16 if p.size>16 else p.size
     idx=np.argpartition(p, -k)[-k:]
     wsum=p[idx].sum()
     if wsum > 0.98:
        grad=np.zeros(n)
        for kidx in idx:  # top-level for? Helper top-level, no lambda; for loop ok.
           w=p[kidx]; kint=int(kidx); if w<1e-12 continue
           j0=max(0,kint-n+1); j1=min(kint,n-1)
           if j1>=j0:
               other=kint - np.arange(j0,j1+1)
               grad[j0:j1+1] += (2.0*w)*a[other]
        return grad
  # else FFT

Constructing smooth random init:
def make_smooth_random(n, rng, amplitude=0.5):
  # Generate Fourier random phases low-pass
  x = rng.standard_normal(n)
  # apply Gaussian low-pass via FFT: filter = exp(-f^2*(2πf/n)^2)
  X=np.fft.rfft(x)
  f=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
  sigman= max(1.0, n/20)
  filt=np.exp(-0.5*(f* n / (n/20))**2)? simpler: filt=np.exp(-0.5*(f/(1/n/5?))**2)
Need top-level.
Better: generate random smooth s by sum of few sinusoids at low frequencies with random phases/amplitudes. E.g., frequencies m=1..F (F~8) random amplitude decaying; s_i=∑ A_m cos(2π m i/n + φ). Scale to desired std (e.g., 0.5) and clip [-1,1.5]. This avoids O(n logn)? We can use loops small.
Function random_sinusoid_s(n, rng, max_mode=12, target_std=0.4):
  s=np.zeros(n)
  amps=rng.random(max_mode); amps*=np.exp(-np.arange(max_mode)*0.5)
  phases=rng.uniform(0,2π,max_mode)
  i=np.arange(n)
  for m in range(1,max_mode+1): s += amps[m-1]*np.cos(2π*m*i/n+phase)
  if std==0 return zeros
  s *= target_std / s.std()
  s -= s.mean()
  s = np.clip(s, -1.0, 1.5)
  # after clipping sum not zero; but a normalize handles.
  return s

"""..."""
import numpy as np
import time

def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def fast_conv(a):
   n=int(a.size)
   if n==0: return np.empty(0)
   if n < 64: return np.convolve(a,a) ? maybe faster. But direct for small.
   M=2*n-1
   L=next_pow2(M)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:M]
   c = np.real(c)
   return c

def norm_reset(a): returns normalized nonnegative sum1; if sum<1e-12 uniform.

def score_from_a(a): n=len; S=sum; if S<1e-12 inf; an=a/S; c=fast_conv(an); return 2*n*c.max()/S*S? Actually if an normalized, 2*n*cmax. Since formula scale invariant; return 2*n*float(c.max()) if S=1. For arbitrary: 2*n*max(c)/S^2. Use arbitrary to verify. But for internal we use normalized.

def s_to_a(s,n): a=(1+s)/n; a=np.maximum(a,0); S=a.sum(); if S<=...; a/=S; return a

def a_to_s(a,n): return n*a - 1.0 (after normalize)

def lag_grad_one(a,k):
  n=len(a); grad=np.zeros(n)
  j0 = k - n + 1
  if j0 < 0: j0=0
  j1 = k
  if j1 >= n: j1 = n-1
  if j1 < j0: return grad
  j = np.arange(j0, j1+1)
  other = k - j
  grad[j0:j1+1] = 2.0 * a[other]
  return grad

def adjoint_fft(a,p):
  n=len(a)
  L2 = 3*n - 2
  Lft=next_pow2(L2)
  A=np.fft.rfft(a,Lft)
  PR=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],Lft)
  conv=np.fft.irfft(A*PR,Lft)[:L2]
  return 2.0 * conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]

def adjoint_auto(a,p):
  n=len(a); L=p.size
  if L==0: return zeros
  pmax = float(p.max())
  if pmax > 0.98:
     # if almost one lag
     max_k = int(np.argmax(p))
     if pmax > 0.999: return lag_grad_one(a,max_k)
     topk = min(16,L)
     idx=np.argpartition(p, -topk)[-topk:]
     wsum=float(p[idx].sum())
     if wsum > 0.98:
        grad=np.zeros(n)
        for idxi in idx:
           w=float(p[idxi]);
           if w <= 1e-14: continue
           k=int(idxi);
           j0=max(0,k-n+1); j1=min(k,n-1)
           if j1>=j0:
              jj=np.arange(j0,j1+1)
              grad[j0:j1+1] += (2.0*w)*a[k-jj]
        return grad
  return adjoint_fft(a,p)

Need ensure top-level; lag_grad_one creates arange each lag; for top 16 ok. If n large, np.arange huge each, memory. Could precompute? Indices per lag not precompute for many. But top few only. OK.

Potential bug: p size L = len(c). adjoint_fft expects p length 2n-1. Good.

def compute_lse(a,n,pe):
  c=fast_conv(a)
  C=n*c
  # Replace nan/inf?
  if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)): fallback
  cmax = float(C.max())
  if cmax <= 0: return 0, zeros(p)
  beta = pe / cmax
  shifted = beta*(C - cmax)
  # To avoid overflow? max 0. min negative
  # But if C has values >cmax due roundoff? cmax max.
  # If beta large and many zeros, exp sum ok.
  e=np.exp(shifted)
  ss=e.sum()
  if ss<=0 or nonfinite: p=zeros; p[int(np.argmax(C))]=1
  else: p=e/ss
  soft = cmax + np.log(ss)/beta if beta>0 else cmax
  grad=adjoint_auto(a,p)
  return cmax, grad

Note if C values tiny negative, C-cmax negative; exp ok. But p sum maybe finite. If cmax=0 (zero sequence) not happen.

Beta dynamic: pe / cmax. If cmax=0.75, pe=30 -> beta=40, scaled range ~30? min 0 => e^-30 ~9e-14, okay. If cmax=2, beta=15, range 30. Good. p focuses top where C within ~1/beta of max, i.e. relative pe/cmax^2? Fine.

def run_s_descent(a_init, t_start, time_limit, lr0=0.10, lr1=0.005, pe0=1.0, pe1=40.0, cap=3.0, seed=None, max_steps=None):
  n=len(a_init)
  s = n*a_init - 1.0
  # ensure sum zero? a normalized.
  best_a = a_init.copy(); best_score = score_from_a(a_init)
  m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); step=0
  run_start = time.monotonic()
  # If time_limit<=0 return
  while True:
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - run_start
    if elapsed >= time_limit or (max_steps and step>=max_steps): break
    frac = min(1.0, elapsed / max(time_limit,1e-9))
    lr = lr0 * (lr1/lr0) ** frac if lr0>lr1 else lr0
    pe = pe0 + (pe1 - pe0) * (1.0 - np.sqrt(1.0 - frac))? We want pe low early quick. Use pe = pe0 + (pe1-pe0)*frac**0.5? At frac0.25 pe ~ pe0+0.5(range). Use pe = pe0 + (pe1-pe0)*frac.
    a = s_to_a(s,n)
    cmax, grad = compute_lse(a,n,pe)
    score = 2.0*cmax
    if score < best_score - 1e-15:
       best_score=score; best_a=a.copy()
    # Center gradient and maybe normalize gradient magnitude? Adam handles.
    grad = np.asarray(grad, dtype=np.float64)
    if not finite: reset? 
    grad -= grad.mean()
    # Optional: project negative? no.
    step += 1
    m_t = 0.9*m +0.1*grad
    v_t = 0.999*v +0.001*grad*grad
    b1=0.9; b2=0.999
    mhat=m_t/(1-b1**step); vhat=v_t/(1-b2**step)
    ds = lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
    s = s - ds
    s = np.clip(s, -1.0, cap)
    a = s_to_a(s,n)
    if step % 100 == 0: # maybe check time? no.
       pass
  return best_a,best_score

Now resampling function:
def resample_a(a, target_n):
  n=len(a)
  if target_n == n: return a.copy()
  # continuous density f_i = a_i * n
  f = a * n
  # Assume support [0,1] endpoints at i/(n-1). For n=1 special
  if n==1: return np.ones(target_n)/target_n
  x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
  x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,target_n)
  f_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,f,left=f[0],right=f[-1])
  a_new=f_new/target_n
  a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0.0)
  S=a_new.sum()
  if S<=0: uniform
  a_new/=S
  return a_new

Maybe interpolation from array with negative? a nonneg. Good.

Random smooth perturbation of s:
def add_smooth_noise(s, rng, sigma, max_mode=12):
  n=len(s)
  modes=max(2,min(max_mode,n//2))
  i=np.arange(n)
  noise=np.zeros(n)
  for m in range(1,modes+1):
     amp=sigma*np.exp(-0.5*(m-1)/max_mode)
     phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
     noise += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*i/n + phase)
  s2=s+noise
  # maybe clip
  return s2
Because we normalize after. Good.

def make_sinusoid_a(n, rng, max_mode=8, target_amp=0.6):
  s=np.zeros(n); ... target mean of s^2 ~0.5? Scale.
  # Ensure nonneg: clip [-0.9,1.5]
  s = np.clip(s, -1.0, 1.5)
  a=s_to_a(s,n)
  return a

def make_random_s(n, rng, small_n=128, max_mode=64):
  # sample random s on small grid, cubic? linear interp
  s_small = rng.standard_normal(small_n)
  # low-pass small: filter freq
  X=np.fft.rfft(s_small); f=np.fft.rfftfreq(small_n); L=10.0; filt=np.exp(-0.5*(f*small_n/L)**2); s_small=irfft(X*filt,len(small_n))
  # scale to std0.5, mean0, clip [-1,1]
  s = interp small to n length; scale; clip.

Let's design make_sinusoid_s more carefully:
def make_sinusoid_s(n, rng, max_mode=16, target_std=0.45):
  s = np.zeros(n)
  i = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
  for m in range(1, max_mode+1):
      amp = rng.random() * np.exp(-0.6*(m-1)/max_mode)
      phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi)
      s += amp * np.cos(2.0*np.pi*m*i/n + phase)
  if s.std() > 1e-12:
      s *= target_std / s.std()
  s -= s.mean()
  s = np.clip(s, -1.0, 1.5)
  return s
This can produce s not zero sum after clip; normalization handles. target_std maybe 0.5 to have average s^2 ~0.25. If uniform initial s=0, target_std 0.5 random. Score maybe not too high. Clip at -1 may cut tails; OK.

def add_smooth_noise(s,rng,sigma,max_mode=32):
  n=len(s); noise=np.zeros(n); i=np.arange(n)
  amode=max(2,min(max_mode,n//2))
  for m in 1..amode: amp=sigma*np.exp(-0.5*(m-1)/max(1,amode)); phase...
  noise += amp cos...
  return s + noise

Now, code for normalize:
def normalize_a(a):
  a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
  a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
  a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
  S = float(a.sum())
  n = a.size
  if n == 0 or S <= 1e-12:
     if n == 0: return np.ones(64)
     return np.ones(n,n dtype)/n
  a = a / S
  return a

Soft = max approx C. p weights. dSoft/ds_j = ∑ p_k ∂(n c_k)/∂s_j. ∂c_k/∂a_j = 2a_other (as before). ∂a_other/∂s_j? a=(1+s)/n without normalization; a_other = (1+s_other)/n; derivative =1/n. s_to_a:
def s_to_a(s,n):
  a=(1.0+s)/float(n)
  a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
  S=a.sum()
  if S <=1e-12: return np.ones(n)/n
  return a/S

add_smooth_noise top-level:
def add_smooth_noise(s, rng, sigma, max_mode=32):
  n=int(s.size)
  if sigm a <=0 or n<4: return s.copy()
  amax=max(2,min(max_mode,int(n//2)))
  i=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
  noise=np.zeros(n)
  for m in range(1,amax+1):
      amp=sigma*np.exp(-0.5*(m-1)/max(1,amax-1 if amax>1 else 1))
      ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
      noise += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*i/ n + ph)
  return s + noise

Note if n large and amax=32, loop 32 with cos O(n) each -> 32*30000=1M ops, fine but if called every 5000 steps? no.

make_sinusoid_s similar but set target std:
def make_sinusoid_s(n,rng,max_mode=24,target_std=0.45):
  s=np.zeros(n)
  i=np.arange(n,float)
  amax=max(2,min(max_mode,n//2))
  for m...
    amp=np.exp(-0.5*(m-1)/amax)
    phase...
    s += amp*cos
  std=s.std()
  if std>1e-14: s *= target_std/std
  s -= s.mean()
  s=np.clip(s,-1.0,1.5)
  return s

resample_a:
def resample_a(a,target_n):
  n=a.size
  if target_n==n: return a.copy()
  if n==1: return np.ones(target_n)/target_n
  x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  x_new=np.linspace(0,1,target_n)
  f=a*float(n)
  f_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,f,left=f[0],right=f[-1])
  a_new=f_new/float(target_n)
  return normalize_a(a_new)

Pseudo:
def run_s_descent(a_init, time_limit, lr0=0.08, lr1=0.004, pe0=1.0, pe1=50.0, cap_s=6.0, seed=0, max_steps=1000000):
  n=a_init.size
  a=normalize_a(a_init)
  s=n*a - 1.0
  best_a=a.copy(); best_s=s.copy(); best_score=2.0*compute_cmax(a,n)? Use function score_cmax.
Define score_cmax(a): c=fast_conv(a); cmax=n*max(c) maybe; return 2*cmax. We'll use in run.
  m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); b1=0.9; b2=0.999; eps=1e-8
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  run_start=time.monotonic()
  step=0; bias1=1.0; bias2=1.0; last_improve=0; stuck=0
  while True:
    elapsed=time.monotonic()-run_start
    if elapsed >= time_limit or step >= max_steps: break
    frac=elapsed/max(time_limit,1e-9)
    if frac>1: frac=1
    lr=lr0*(lr1/lr0)**frac if lr0>lr1 else lr0
    pe=pe0+(pe1-pe0)*frac
    # if stuck, maybe lower pe? 
    cmax, grad = compute_cmax_grad(a, n, pe)  # uses a as state (sum1)
    score=2.0*cmax
    if score < best_score - 1e-13:
       best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); best_s=s.copy(); last_improve=step; stuck=0
    else:
       stuck += 1
    if stuck > 8000 and time_limit - elapsed > 90:
        s = add_smooth_noise(best_s, rng, 0.15, 32)
        a = s_to_a(s,n)
        s = n*a - 1.0
        m.fill(0); v.fill(0); bias1=1.0; bias2=1.0; stuck=0
        # maybe continue with this a; next loop computes.
        # To avoid immediate recompute? OK.
    # update
    grad = np.asarray(grad,float)
    if not all finite: break
    grad -= grad.mean()
    step += 1
    bias1 *= b1; bias2 *= b2
    m = b1*m + (1-b1)*grad
    v = b2*v + (1-b2)*grad*grad
    mhat=m/(1-bias1); vhat=v/(1-bias2)
    ds=lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
    s = s - ds
    s = np.clip(s, -1.0, cap_s)
    a_new = s_to_a(s,n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a_new)): break
    a = a_new
    s = n*a - 1.0
  return a,best_score

Here compute_cmax_grad(a,n,pe) uses a normalized. We need ensure a sum1 each loop. It should.

Function compute_cmax_grad:
def compute_cmax_grad(a,n,pe):
  c=fast_conv(a)
  c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
  C=c*float(n)
  cmax=float(C.max())
  if cmax <= 1e-15:
     return 0.0, np.zeros(n)
  beta=pe/cmax
  if beta < 1e-12: beta=1e-12
  if beta > 1e7: beta=1e7
  shifted=beta*(C-cmax)
  e=np.exp(shifted)
  ss=e.sum()
  if ss <=0 or not finite:
     p=np.zeros(2*n-1); p[int(np.argmax(C))]=1.0
  else: p=e/ss
  grad=adjoint_auto(a,p)
  return cmax, grad

  return out_list

Implementation of adjoint_sparse:
  grad=np.zeros(n)
  for idx in idx:
    w=float(p[idx])
    if w <= 1e-14: continue
    k=int(idx)
    j0=max(0,k-n+1); j1=min(k,n-1)
    if j1>=j0:
       # use slice assignment with reversed segment
       # other = k - np.arange(j0,j1+1)
       # Can avoid arange by using slices with negative step:
       # If k < n: j0=0, j1=k, other = k, k-1,...0 => a[k:k-1:-1] if k>0 else a[0:1]
       # If k >= n: j0=k-n+1, j1=n-1, other = n-1 down? other_start=k - j0 = n-1, other_end=k - j1 maybe >=
       # General with arange is simpler; for top <=16,n<=32768, arange length n each => 0.5M ints; memory ok but time. Could use slice for speed.
       if k < n:
           seg = a[k:-1:-1] if k>0 else a[0:1]
       else:
           # j0 = k-n+1; j1=n-1; other = n-1 down to k-(n-1)=k-n+1
           start = n-1
           stop = k-n+1 - 1? Need descending from n-1 to stop_other.
           # other list: n-1, n-2, ..., k-n+1. Length = n - (k-n+1) = 2n-1-k.
           # a[n-1:k-n:-1]
           seg = a[n-1:k-n:-1]
       grad[j0:j1+1] += (2.0*w)*seg
  return grad

def fast_conv(a):
 n=a.size
 if n<0? If n==0 return empty
 if n < 128:
    return np.convolve(a, a)

 A=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
 c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)[:2*n-1]
 # Remove tiny imaginary? irfft real.
 return c

s_to_a:
def s_to_a(s,n):
  n=int(n)
  a=(1.0+np.asarray(s,dtype=np.float64))/float(n)
  a=np.maximum(a,0)
  S=float(a.sum())
  if S<=1e-12: return np.ones(n)/n
  return a/S

add_smooth_noise:
def add_smooth_noise(s, rng, sigma, max_mode=32):
  s=np.asarray(s,dtype=np.float64)
  n=s.size
  out=s.copy()
  if n<8 or sigma<=0: return out
  amax=int(max(2,min(max_mode, n//2)))
  i=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
  noise=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
  for m in range(1,amax+1):
     decay=max(1,amax-1)
     amp=sigma*np.exp(-0.5*(m-1)/decay)
     ph=float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi))
     noise += amp*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*float(m)*i/float(n) + ph)
  out += noise
  return out

make_sinusoid_s:
def make_sinusoid_s(n,rng,max_mode=24,target_std=0.5):
  n=int(n)
  s=np.zeros(n)
  if n<8: return s
  amax=int(max(2,min(max_mode,n//2)))
  i=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
  for m... similar
  std=float(s.std())
  if std>1e-14: s *= target_std/std
  s -= s.mean()
  s = np.clip(s, -1.0, 1.5)
  return s

resample_a:
def resample_a(a,target_n):
  a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
  n=a.size
  target_n=int(target_n)
  if target_n<=0: return np.ones(64)/64
  if target_n==n: return a.copy()
  if n==1: return np.ones(target_n)/target_n
  o=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
  nu=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,target_n)
  f=a*float(n)
  fn=np.interp(nu,o,f,left=float(f[0]),right=float(f[-1]))
  an=fn/float(target_n)
  return normalize_a(an)

compute_cmax_grad:
def compute_cmax_grad(a,n,pe):
  n=int(n)
  a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
  c=fast_conv(a)
  if c.size == 2*n-1:
     c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
  else:
     c=np.zeros(2*n-1); L=min(c.size,2*n-1); c[:L]=np.maximum(c[:L],0.0)
  C=c*float(n)
  if C.size==0: return 0, zeros
  if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
     C=np.nan_to_num(C, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
  cmax=float(C.max()) if C.size else 0.0
  if cmax <= 1e-15:
     return 0.0, np.zeros(n)
  beta=float(pe)/cmax
  if beta < 1e-12: beta=1e-12
  if beta > 1e7: beta=1e7
  shifted=beta*(C-cmax)
  e=np.exp(shifted)
  ss=float(e.sum())
  if ss <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ss):
     p=np.zeros(C.size)
     p[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
  else:
     p=e/ss
     if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
        p=np.zeros(C.size); p[int(np.argmax(C))]=1.0
  return cmax, adjoint_auto(a,p)

`np.argmax(C)` if C all zeros? cmax> so fine.

adjoint_auto:
def adjoint_auto(a,p):
  n=int(a.size)
  grad=np.zeros(n)
  if n<=0: return grad
  if p.size != 2*n-1:
      # fallback? if too short pad
      tmp=np.zeros(2*n-1); m=min(p.size,2*n-1); tmp[:m]=p[:m]; p=tmp
  pmax=float(np.max(p)) if p.size else 0.0
  if not np.isfinite(pmax): return grad
  if pmax > 0.95:
      topk = min(16, p.size)
      if topk >= p.size:
          idx=np.nonzero(p > 0.0)[0]
      else:
          idx=np.argpartition(p, -topk)[-topk:]
      wsum=float(np.sum(p[idx])) if idx.size else 0.0
      if wsum > 0.98:
          for idxi in idx:
              w=float(p[idxi])
              if w <= 1e-14: continue
              k=int(idxi)
              if k < n:
                  j0=0; j1=k
                  if k > 0:
                     seg=a[k:-1:-1]
                  else:
                     seg=a[0:1]
              else:
                  j0=k-n+1; j1=n-1
                  # stop = k-n (exclusive)
                  seg=a[n-1:k-n:-1]
              if seg.size > 0 and j1>=j0:
                  grad[j0:j1+1] += (2.0*w)*seg
          return grad
  return adjoint_fft(a,p)

Check slice for k>=n: If k=2n-2, stop=n-2, slice a[n-1:n-2:-1] gives [n-1]. j0=n-1. Good. If k=n, stop=0, slice a[n-1:0:-1] gives n-1..1, j0=1 length n-1. Good. If k=n-? handled.
If k< n and k=0, seg=a[0:1], j0=0,j1=0. Good.

Potential issue: If p has top weights, but p not normalized? It is normalized. If wsum>0.98, good.

adjoint_fft:
def adjoint_fft(a,p):
  n=int(a.size)
  if n<=0 or p.size != 2*n-1:
      # if p length mismatch, pad/trunc
      tmp=np.zeros(2*n-1)
      m=min(p.size, 2*n-1)
      tmp[:m]=p[:m]
      p=tmp
  L2=3*n-2
  L=next_pow2(L2)
  A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
  PR=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],L)
  conv=np.fft.irfft(A*PR,L)[:L2]
  return 2.0*conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]

If n=1: L2=1; slice n-1=0:2 -> conv len1 slice 0:2 [c0] reversed ok. p size=1.

run_s_descent:
def run_s_descent(a_init, time_limit, lr0=0.06, lr1=0.003, pe0=2.0, pe1=80.0, cap_s=8.0, seed=0, max_steps=20000000):
  a=normalize_a(a_init)
  n=a.size
  if time_limit <= 0 or n==0:
      return a, score_sequence(a)
  s=n*a - 1.0
  best_a=a.copy(); best_s=s.copy()
  best_score=score_sequence(a)
  m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
  b1=0.9; b2=0.999; eps=1e-8
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  start=time.monotonic()
  step=0; bias1=1.0; bias2=1.0; last_imp=0; stuck=0
  while True:
    elapsed=time.monotonic()-start
    if elapsed >= time_limit or step >= int(max_steps): break
    frac=elapsed / max(float(time_limit), 1e-9)
    if frac >1: frac=1.0
    lr=float(lr0) * (float(lr1)/float(lr0)) ** frac if lr0 > lr1 else float(lr0)
    pe=float(pe0) + (float(pe1)-float(pe0))*frac
    cmax, grad = compute_cmax_grad(a,n,pe)
    score=2.0*cmax
    if score < best_score - 1e-13:
       best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); best_s=s.copy(); last_imp=step
    elif step - last_imp > 8000 and (time_limit - elapsed) > 90:
       # perturb
       sig=0.12 if n < 10000 else 0.18
       s = add_smooth_noise(best_s, rng, sig, 32)
       a = s_to_a(s,n)
       s = n*a - 1.0
       m.fill(0.0); v.fill(0.0); bias1=1.0; bias2=1.0; last_imp=step
       # continue to next iteration? We can continue without gradient? We need go to top.
       continue
    if step - last_imp > 8000: # even if no time to perturb, maybe reduce lr? But lr decays.
       lr *= 0.5
    grad=np.asarray(grad,dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(grad).all(): break
    grad -= grad.mean()
    step +=1
    bias1 *= b1; bias2 *= b2
    m = b1*m + (1.0-b1)*grad
    v = b2*v + (1.0-b2)*grad*grad
    mhat = m/(1.0-bias1)
    vhat = v/(1.0-bias2)
    ds = lr * mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
    s = s - ds
    s = np.clip(s, -1.0, float(cap_s))
    a_new = s_to_a(s,n)
    if not np.isfinite(a_new).all():
       break
    a = a_new
    s = n*a - 1.0
  return a,best_score

If no perturb due time but step-last_imp>8000, lr*=0.5 each step after, good.

```python
"""
Search algorithm:
  - Work with normalized non-negative sequences a (sum(a)=1).
  - Objective is 2 * n * max(conv(a,a)) = 2 * max(n * conv(a,a)).
  - Parameterize a_i = (1 + s_i) / n, so s >= -1 and sum(s)=0 when a isnormalized.
  - Use a smooth LSE approximation to the maximum of the convolution coefficients,
    with the beta scale chosen relative to the current maximum coefficient.
  - Bound-gradient Adam-style optimization in the O(1) coefficient space s,
    with periodic random smooth restarts, multi-length resampling, and random
    sinusoidal initializations to escape local minima.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def fast_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n < 128:
        return np.convolve(a, a).astype(np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    L = next_pow2(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:m]
    return c.astype(np.float64)


def normalize_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / S


def score_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    n = a.size
    a = normalize_a(a)
    c = fast_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if cm <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cm):
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * float(n) * cm


def s_to_a(s, n):
    n = int(n)
    s = np.asarray(s, dtype=np.float64)
    a = (1.0 + s) / float(n)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / S


def add_smooth_noise(s, rng, sigma, max_mode=32):
    s = np.asarray(s, dtype=np.float64)
    out = s.copy()
    n = s.size
    if n < 8 or sigma <= 0.0:
        return out
    amax = int(max(2, min(max_mode, n // 2)))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    noise = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    decay = max(1, amax - 1)
    for m in range(1, amax + 1):
        amp = float(sigma) * np.exp(-0.5 * (m - 1) / float(decay))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        noise += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * i / float(n) + ph)
    out += noise
    return out


def make_sinusoid_s(n, rng, max_mode=24, target_std=0.50):
    n = int(n)
    s = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n < 8:
        return s
    amax = int(max(2, min(max_mode, n // 2)))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    decay = max(1, amax - 1)
    for m in range(1, amax + 1):
        amp = np.exp(-0.5 * (m - 1) / float(decay))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        s += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * i / float(n) + ph)
    std = float(s.std())
    if std > 1e-14:
        s *= float(target_std) / std
    s -= float(s.mean())
    s = np.clip(s, -1.0, 1.5)
    return s


def resample_a(a, target_n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    target_n = int(target_n)
    if target_n <= 0:
        return np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0
    if target_n == n:
        return a.copy()
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(target_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(target_n)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, target_n, dtype=np.float64)
    f = a * float(n)
    fn = np.interp(xn, xo, f, left=float(f[0]), right=float(f[-1]))
    an = fn / float(target_n)
    return normalize_a(an)


def adjoint_fft(a, p):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    if p.size != need:
        tmp = np.zeros(need, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(p.size, need)
        if m > 0:
            tmp[:m] = p[:m]
        p = tmp
    L2 = 3 * n - 2
    L = next_pow2(L2)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    PR = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], L)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * PR, L)[:L2]
    return 2.0 * conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]


def adjoint_auto(a, p):
    n = int(a.size)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return grad

    need = 2 * n - 1
    if p.size != need:
        tmp = np.zeros(need, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(p.size, need)
        if m > 0:
            tmp[:m] = p[:m]
        p = tmp

    pmax = float(np.max(p)) if p.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(pmax):
        return grad

    # If the LSE weights are effectively a few active lags, compute the
    # exact sparse gradient directly; otherwise use FFT.
    if pmax > 0.95:
        topk = min(16, p.size)
        if topk >= p.size:
            idx = np.nonzero(p > 0.0)[0]
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(p, -topk)[-topk:]
        wsum = float(np.sum(p[idx])) if idx.size else 0.0
        if wsum > 0.98:
            for idxi in idx:
                w = float(p[idxi])
                if w <= 1e-14:
                    continue
                k = int(idxi)
                if k < n:
                    j0 = 0
                    j1 = k
                    if k > 0:
                        seg = a[k:-1:-1]
                    else:
                        seg = a[0:1]
                else:
                    j0 = k - n + 1
                    j1 = n - 1
                    seg = a[n - 1:k - n:-1]
                if j1 >= j0 and seg.size > 0:
                    grad[j0:j1 + 1] += (2.0 * w) * seg
            return grad

    return adjoint_fft(a, p)


def compute_cmax_grad(a, n, pe):
    n = int(n)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    c = fast_conv(a)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    if c.size != need:
        tmp = np.zeros(need, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(c.size, need)
        if m > 0:
            tmp[:m] = c[:m]
        c = tmp
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    C = c * float(n)
    C = np.nan_to_num(C, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    cmax = float(np.max(C)) if C.size else 0.0
    if cmax <= 1e-15:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(pe) / cmax
    if beta < 1e-12:
        beta = 1e-12
    if beta > 1e7:
        beta = 1e7

    shifted = beta * (C - cmax)
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    ss = float(e.sum())
    if ss <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ss):
        p = np.zeros(C.size, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
    else:
        p = e / ss
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            p = np.zeros(C.size, dtype=np.float64)
            p[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0

    return cmax, adjoint_auto(a, p)


def run_s_descent(a_init, time_limit, lr0=0.06, lr1=0.003,
                  pe0=2.0, pe1=80.0, cap_s=8.0, seed=0, max_steps=20000000):
    a = normalize_a(a_init)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, np.inf
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return a, score_sequence(a)

    s = float(n) * a - 1.0
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_s = s.copy()
    best_score = score_sequence(a)

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b1 = 0.9
    b2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    start = time.monotonic()
    step = 0
    bias1 = 1.0
    bias2 = 1.0
    last_imp = 0

    while True:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        if elapsed >= float(time_limit) or step >= int(max_steps):
            break

        frac = elapsed / max(float(time_limit), 1e-9)
        if frac > 1.0:
            frac = 1.0

        lr = float(lr0)
        if float(lr0) > float(lr1):
            lr = float(lr0) * (float(lr1) / float(lr0)) ** frac
        else:
            lr = float(lr0)
        pe = float(pe0) + (float(pe1) - float(pe0)) * frac

        cmax, grad = compute_cmax_grad(a, n, pe)
        current_score = 2.0 * cmax

        if np.isfinite(current_score) and current_score < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = float(current_score)
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_s = s.copy()
            last_imp = step
        elif step - last_imp > 8000 and (float(time_limit) - elapsed) > 90.0:
            sig = 0.12 if n < 10000 else 0.18
            s = add_smooth_noise(best_s, rng, sig, 32)
            a = s_to_a(s, n)
            s = float(n) * a - 1.0
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            bias1 = 1.0
            bias2 = 1.0
            last_imp = step
            continue

        if step - last_imp > 8000:
            lr *= 0.5

        grad = np.asarray(grad, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            break

        # Tangentialize to the sum-zero constraint in s-space.
        grad -= float(grad.mean())

        step += 1
        bias1 *= b1
        bias2 *= b2
        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * grad
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (grad * grad)
        mhat = m / (1.0 - bias1)
        vhat = v / (1.0 - bias2)
        ds = lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)

        s = s - ds
        s = np.clip(s, -1.0, float(cap_s))
        a_new = s_to_a(s, n)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a_new)):
            break
        a = a_new
        s = float(n) * a - 1.0

    return best_a, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    effective = max(30.0, float(budget_s)) - 45.0
    if effective < 90.0:
        effective = 90.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_score = np.inf
    best_a = None
    parent_a = None

    parent_raw = None
    try:
        if 'height_sequence_1' in globals():
            parent_raw = np.asarray(globals()['height_sequence_1'], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        parent_raw = None

    if parent_raw is not None and parent_raw.size >= 16:
        a = normalize_a(parent_raw)
        sc = score_sequence(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            parent_a = a.copy()
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_score = sc
            print(f"Parent n={a.size}, score={best_score:.10f}")

    if best_a is None:
        a = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64) / 4096.0
        sc = score_sequence(a)
        best_a = a.copy()
        best_score = sc
        parent_a = a.copy()
        print(f"No usable parent, uniform n={a.size}, score={best_score:.10f}")

    print(f"Initial best score = {best_score:.10f}, target <= 1.5027")

    # 1) Primary optimization on the current best length.
    remaining = effective - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if remaining > 180.0 and best_a is not None:
        alloc = min(750.0, remaining - 30.0)
        n = best_a.size
        lr0 = 0.06 if n < 10000 else 0.04
        a_out, sc = run_s_descent(
            best_a, alloc,
            lr0=lr0, lr1=0.002,
            pe0=2.0, pe1=80.0,
            cap_s=8.0, seed=12345
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a_out.copy()
            print(f"Primary optimization: {best_score:.10f}, n={best_a.size}")

    # 2) Resample to a larger length and optimize.
    remaining = effective - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if remaining > 250.0 and best_a is not None:
        nbase = best_a.size
        target = 12288
        if nbase > 10000:
            target = int(max(target, int(1.2 * nbase)))
        if target > nbase:
            a_init = resample_a(best_a, target)
            sc_init = score_sequence(a_init)
            if np.isfinite(sc_init) and sc_init < best_score:
                best_score = sc_init
                best_a = a_init.copy()
            alloc = min(500.0, remaining - 30.0)
            a_out, sc = run_s_descent(
                a_init, alloc,
                lr0=0.05, lr1=0.002,
                pe0=4.0, pe1=100.0,
                cap_s=8.0, seed=54321
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a_out.copy()
                print(f"Resampled opt n={a_out.size}: {best_score:.10f}")

    # 3) Smooth-noise restart from the best sequence.
    remaining = effective - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if remaining > 150.0 and best_a is not None:
        n = best_a.size
        s = float(n) * best_a - 1.0
        s2 = add_smooth_noise(s, rng, 0.12, 24)
        a_init = s_to_a(s2, n)
        sc_init = score_sequence(a_init)
        if np.isfinite(sc_init) and sc_init < best_score:
            best_score = sc_init
            best_a = a_init.copy()
        alloc = min(240.0, remaining - 30.0)
        a_out, sc = run_s_descent(
            a_init, alloc,
            lr0=0.08, lr1=0.003,
            pe0=1.0, pe1=80.0,
            cap_s=8.0, seed=98765
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a_out.copy()
            print(f"Noise restart opt: {best_score:.10f}, n={best_a.size}")

    # 4) Second larger resampled length.
    remaining = effective - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if remaining > 300.0 and best_a is not None and best_a.size < 24576:
        a_init = resample_a(best_a, 24576)
        sc_init = score_sequence(a_init)
        if np.isfinite(sc_init) and sc_init < best_score:
            best_score = sc_init
            best_a = a_init.copy()
        alloc = min(400.0, remaining - 30.0)
        a_out, sc = run_s_descent(
            a_init, alloc,
            lr0=0.045, lr1=0.0018,
            pe0=4.0, pe1=110.0,
            cap_s=8.0, seed=31415
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a_out.copy()
            print(f"Large n=24576 opt: {best_score:.10f}")

    # 5) Random sinusoidal initialization.
    remaining = effective - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if remaining > 200.0:
        n_rand = 16384
        s = make_sinusoid_s(n_rand, rng, max_mode=24, target_std=0.50)
        a_init = s_to_a(s, n_rand)
        sc_init = score_sequence(a_init)
        print(f"Random sinusoid init n={n_rand}: {sc_init:.10f}")
        if np.isfinite(sc_init) and sc_init < best_score:
            best_score = sc_init
            best_a = a_init.copy()
        alloc = min(250.0, remaining - 30.0)
        a_out, sc = run_s_descent(
            a_init, alloc,
            lr0=0.12, lr1=0.02,
            pe0=0.5, pe1=70.0,
            cap_s=10.0, seed=int(seed + 7919)
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a_out.copy()
            print(f"Random init opt: {best_score:.10f}, n={best_a.size}")

    # 6) Final high-beta polish.
    remaining = effective - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if remaining > 120.0 and best_a is not None:
        alloc = min(200.0, remaining - 20.0)
        a_out, sc = run_s_descent(
            best_a, alloc,
            lr0=0.015, lr1=0.0005,
            pe0=60.0, pe1=300.0,
            cap_s=6.0, seed=777
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a_out.copy()
            print(f"Final polish: {best_score:.10f}, n={best_a.size}")

    # Final sanitize and return.
    best_a = normalize_a(best_a)
    final_internal = score_sequence(best_a)
    if not np.isfinite(final_internal):
        if parent_a is not None:
            best_a = normalize_a(parent_a)
        else:
            best_a = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64) / 4096.0
        final_internal = score_sequence(best_a)

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a]
    if sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [1.0] * 64

    verified = np.inf
    if best_a.size <= 30000:
        try:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        except Exception:
            verified = final_internal

    if not np.isfinite(verified) and parent_a is not None:
        out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in normalize_a(parent_a)]
        try:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        except Exception:
            verified = score_sequence(np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64))

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print(f"FINAL internal={final_internal:.10f}, verified={verified:.10f}, "
          f"n={len(out)}, time={elapsed:.1f}s/{budget_s}s")

    return out
```
